| 312 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.129_MEMORY_EMULATION.md` | `136791.htm` | #16 | 合并格 | `\| I/O \| Parameter \| Description \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| I \| memoryImageName \| The name of the memory image that is to be compared with the actual ERAM content. \| The name of the memory image that is to be compared with the actual ERAM content. \|⏎\| I \| eramOffset \| The first 32-bit ERAM word that is to be compared \| The first 32-bit ERAM word that is to be compared \|⏎\| I \| eramWords \| The number of 32-bit ERAM words that are to be compared \| The number of 32-bit ERAM words that are to be compared \|⏎\| O \| firstMismatch \| The first ERAM word that does not match the corresponding word of the specified memory image. \| The first ERAM word that does not match the corresponding word of the specified memory image. \|⏎\| O \| lastMismatch \| The last ERAM word that does not match the corresponding word of the specified memory image. \| The last ERAM word that does not match the corresponding word of the specified memory image. \|⏎\| O \| numberOfMismatches \| The number of mismatches in the compared part of ERAM. \| The number of mismatches in the compared part of ERAM. \|⏎\| I \| maskMode \| With this parameter, you can mask bits of an ERAM word so that they will not contribute to the PASS/FAIL result for this word. Possible values are: \| With this parameter, you can mask bits of an ERAM word so that they will not contribute to the PASS/FAIL result for this word. Possible values are: \|⏎\|  \|  \| NO_MASK \| No bit will be masked. This is the default. \|⏎\|  \|  \| WORD_MASK \| The same mask will be applied to all compared words. In this case you must specify the mask with the wordMask parameter. The bits of the value words that correspond to a set bit of the mask will be ignored for the PASS/FAIL result. \|⏎\|  \|  \| IMAGE_MASK \| An individual mask is specified for every expected value word. This word specific mask is defined in the same memory image that also supplies the expected values. This image must in this case have twice the size of the ERAM segment to be compared. The words with an even address (starting from 0) contain the expected values. The words with an odd address contain the mask for the preceding expected value word. The bits of the value word that correspond to a set bit of the mask will be ignored for the PASS/FAIL result. \|⏎\| I \| wordMask \| This parameter specifies the mask for the WORD_MASK mask mode. The default is 0, which means that no bit will be masked. \| This parameter specifies the mask for the WORD_MASK mask mode. The default is 0, which means that no bit will be masked. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/136791.htm`。` |

This option is used to perform a DC measurement with applying a break vector defined in the primary set. To activate the primaries specified with the PRIMARY_STATUS API or primary analog set, put the FLUSH API prior to this statement.<br><br>You can retrieve the result of the functional test with the [GET_FUNCTIONAL_RESULT()](<02.07.04.01.88_GET_FUNCTIONAL_RESULT.md>) API or [TEST](<02.07.04.01.241_TEST.md>) API. \|⏎\| reAct \| "NPRM" \| This option defines no pre-action. | 319 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.151_PMU_IFVM.md` | `29543.htm` | #9 | 合并格 | `\| mode \| Serial / Parallel measurement [1)](<#fntarg_1>) \| AC Relay Control [2)](<#fntarg_2>) \| PMU Relay Control \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| "NP" \| Parallel \| The AC relay of the specified pin is opened during the measurement to disconnect the driver/comparator /active-load.<br><br>After the measurement, the AC relay is closed.<br><br>This mode is for a general DC measurement. \| The PMU is connected to the pogo pin during the measurement. After the measurement, the PMU is disconnected from the pogo pin.<br><br>After the measurement, the PPMU relay is opened for the PPMU. \|⏎\| "NS" \| Serial \| The AC relay of the specified pin is opened during the measurement to disconnect the driver/comparator /active-load.<br><br>After the measurement, the AC relay is closed.<br><br>This mode is for a general DC measurement. \| The PMU is connected to the pogo pin during the measurement. After the measurement, the PMU is disconnected from the pogo pin.<br><br>After the measurement, the PPMU relay is opened for the PPMU. \|⏎\| "TP" \| Parallel \| No change.<br><br>Use this mode to perform a measurement while keeping the AC relay closed. After the measurement, the PMU is disconnected from the pogo pin.<br><br>After the measurement, the PPMU relay is opened for the PPMU. \|⏎\| "SP" \| Parallel \| No change.<br><br>Use this mode to perform a continuity test. SP does a single polarity test, and BP does a both-polarities test. The AC relay should be opened by executing the DISCONNECT sequence beforehand. If the tester is in the disconnect state, the DPS pins are 0 V, open, or the DPS high-impedance mode output; the setting is specified by the "offcurr" parameter of the DPS ("act" sets 0 V, "open" sets open, and "min" sets the DPS high-impedance mode output).<br><br>If the tester is in the disconnect state, during the measurement, the DPS pins are set to 0 V, and the current limit of each DPS is set to the one specified in the level-related primary sets. After the measurement, the PMU is disconnected from the pogo pin.<br><br>After the measurement, the PPMU relay is opened for the PPMU. \|⏎\| "BP" \| Parallel \| No change.<br><br>Use this mode to perform a continuity test. SP does a single polarity test, and BP does a both-polarities test. The AC relay should be opened by executing the DISCONNECT sequence beforehand. If the tester is in the disconnect state, the DPS pins are 0 V, open, or the DPS high-impedance mode output; the setting is specified by the "offcurr" parameter of the DPS ("act" sets 0 V, "open" sets open, and "min" sets the DPS high-impedance mode output).<br><br>If the tester is in the disconnect state, during the measurement, the DPS pins are set to 0 V, and the current limit of each DPS is set to the one specified in the level-related primary sets. That is, the DPSs are set to the same settings as for "act". \| The PMU is connected to the pogo pin during the measurement. Possible values are: \|⏎\|  \|  \| TM::LOOP_OFF \| The transaction sequence is not converted into a match loop. This is the default. \|⏎\|  \|  \| TM::CONTINUE_ON_PASS \| The transaction sequence is converted into a match loop. If the match loop fails, the surrounding test will also be considered as failed. \|⏎\|  \|  \| TM::CONTINUE_ON_FAIL \| The transaction sequence is converted into a match loop. If the match loop fails, the surrounding test will nonetheless be continued. \|⏎\| I \| numLoopIterations \| This parameter defines the maximum number of loop repetitions. If the match loop has not passed with the last of these repetitions it will be considered as failed. \| This parameter defines the maximum number of loop repetitions. | 397 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.43.07_DPS_status_flags_-_get...___functions.md` | `150054.htm` | #1 | 合并格 | `\| Flag \|  \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| High voltage or current of surge tracker exceeded \|  \| The surge tracker has detected a voltage or current above the user defined upper limits. \|⏎\| Low voltage or current of surge tracker exceeded \|  \| The surge tracker has detected a voltage or current below the user defined lower limits. \|⏎\| Measurement range change failed \|  \| A range event in dynamic DC tests has failed. Down ranging is not possible. \|⏎\| Clamp current correction failed \|  \| The clamp current correction for higher accuracy has failed. \|⏎\| Battery full \|  \| The flag indicates the charge condition of the on-board energy storage. The related bit is set if the on-board energy storage is fully charged.<br><br>Note: The flag indicates like the other flags the latched status of the on-board energy storage. So even the flag is set, the on-board energy storage may be in a discharged state. We recommend querying the flag twice. With the first query the flag is cleared and the second query will then obtain the actual state. \|⏎\| Power budget 1 \|  \| The flags indicate that the utilization of the related power budget has entered a critical condition at run time since the last query. \|⏎\| Power budget 2 \|  \| The flags indicate that the utilization of the related power budget has entered a critical condition at run time since the last query. \|⏎\| Power budget 3 \|  \| The flags indicate that the utilization of the related power budget has entered a critical condition at run time since the last query. \|⏎\| High voltage of surge tracker reached \|  \| The flags are used for VI surge tracking mode. The surge tracker has detected a voltage or current above(below) the user defined upper(lower) limits. \|⏎\| Low voltage of surge tracker reached \|  \| The flags are used for VI surge tracking mode. The surge tracker has detected a voltage or current above(below) the user defined upper(lower) limits. \|⏎\| High current of surge tracker reached \|  \| The flags are used for VI surge tracking mode. The surge tracker has detected a voltage or current above(below) the user defined upper(lower) limits. \|⏎\| Low current of surge tracker reached \|  \| The flags are used for VI surge tracking mode. | 598 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.05.01.05_JitterSeparationHS.md` | `148409.htm` | #1 | 合并格 | `\| Group \| Parameter \| Type \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| OperationMode \| OperationMode.productionMode \| string \| Selects the operation mode.The optional values are ON (fast data acquisition) and OFF (full data acquisition). \|⏎\| OperationMode \| OperationMode.transitionSearch \| string \| Enables or disables the transition search. The optional values are ON and OFF. \|⏎\| PinInformation \| PinInformation.pinList \| String \| The list of the pin or pin groups that the spec is shifted for. The member of pin list needs to be O or IO digital pins or pin groups, which are delimited by commas. For differential pins, you only need to input the positive pins. \|⏎\| PinInformation \| PinInformation.portName \| string \| Optional. The port name to which the pinList belongs to. Default value is @. Only pins belonging to one port can be measured together. \|⏎\| PinInformation \| PinInformation.specName_postfix \| string \| The postfix is used to define the spec variables. A spec variable corresponding to each tested pin must be defined to be used for jitter measurement.<br><br>For example, if "Tx0" is the pin to be measured, and the spec variable to use is "Tx0_offset". Then this parameter needs to be set as "offset". \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.UI_width \| double \| The time interval of a bit in the test pattern. It is in the format of value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.leftTransition.start \| double \| - If OperationMode.transitionSearch is OFF, this parameter is the data acquisition start point.<br>- If OperationMode.transitionSearch is ON, this parameter is the transition search start point.<br><br>It is in the format value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.leftTransition.stop \| double \| - If OperationMode.transitionSearch is OFF, this parameter is the data acquisition start point.<br>- If OperationMode.transitionSearch is ON, this parameter is the transition search start point.<br><br>It is in the format value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.rightTransition.start \| double \| - If OperationMode.transitionSearch is OFF, this parameter is the data acquisition start point.<br>- If OperationMode.transitionSearch is ON, this parameter is the transition search start point.<br><br>It is in the format value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.rightTransition.stop \| double \| - If OperationMode.transitionSearch is OFF, this parameter is the data acquisition start point.<br>- If OperationMode.transitionSearch is ON, this parameter is the transition search start point.<br><br>It is in the format value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| DataAcquisition \| DataAcquisition.dataAcquStepWidth \| double \| Step width of data acquisition. it is in the format of value[unit] and the default value is 0[ns]. \|⏎\| Pattern \| Pattern.comparePatternSource \| string \| Specifies whether vector based compare pattern or the hardware PRBS checker is used for pattern comparison:<br><br>- PATTERN_MEMORY - vector based compare pattern.<br>- PRBS_CHECKER - vector based hardware PRBS checker. \|⏎\| Pattern \| Pattern.bitNumbers \| long \| The total bit numbers of the pattern. You must specify the correct total bit numbers of the pattern.<br><br>Note bitNumbers is mandatory for production mode (fast data acquisition). \|⏎\| Pattern \| Pattern.patternType \| string \| Selects whether data (DATA) or clock (CLK) pattern is compared. Where:<br><br>- DATA:- scales maximum error count to bit error rate of 0.5 for data pattern.<br>- CLK: scales maximum error count to bit error rate of 1 for clock pattern.<br>Note The default value is DATA.<br><br>Notice that, the parameter patternType will be disabled when Pattern.bitNumbers is greater than 0 for PATTERN_MEMORY. Also, if PRBS_CHECKER is selected, then patternType will be set to DATA and disabled. \|⏎\| Result \| Result.fittingRangeMax \| double \| The maximum BER number for fitting. \|⏎\| Result \| Result.fittingRangeMin \| double \| The minimum BER number for fitting. \|⏎\| Result \| Result.BERFactor \| int \| The target BER for total jitter calculation. \|⏎\|  \| output.mode \| string \| This parameter is in effect only if output is set to ReportUI. There are three options:<br><br>- SUMMARY: a result table will be printed in the report window.<br>- DETAIL: an ASCII plot of the Error Count and the jitter stardust will be printed in report window additionally.<br>- ANALYSIS: the jitter stardust data will be transfer to signal analyzer tool for display and debugging.<br>Note The default value is SUMMARY. \|⏎\|  \| output \| string \| Selects to wether print a message in the ReportUI window or not:<br><br>- ReportUI: for debug mode<br>- None: for production mode<br>Note The default is ReportUI. \|⏎\|  \| testName \| string \| The test names to be used as limits. The default test name is (random_jitter_ps,deterministic_jitter_ps,total_jitter_ps).<br><br>If you want to use the test names defined in the test table or testflow limits, type in the test names manually. Make sure the test names are in the Test name column of the test table (Refer to [Structure of a TMLimits CSV file](<../02.10_Data_Logging/02.10.01.08.01.01.03.01_Structure_of_a_TMLimits_CSV_file.md>)) or in the Test Name column of Tests page of Flow Data Editor. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/148409.htm`。` |

If extraFailureFlag is set to true, the FAIL_MAP field of the STR record reports 1 - extra failing cycles happened.<br><br>**: Pin 1 stops at the same cycle as pin 2 (failing cycle 7, the 1st lowest cycle that reaches the maxFailsPerPin limit). All failing cycles larger than 25 are discarded so no data is logged for pin 2. \| *: Only the first 5 failing cycles are logged. All failing cycles that come after them are discarded. All failing cycles larger than 25 are discarded so no data is logged for pin 2. \| *: Only the first 5 failing cycles are logged. All failing cycles that come after them are discarded. If extraFailureFlag is set to true, the FAIL_MAP field of the STR record reports 1 - extra failing cycles happened.<br><br>**: Pin 1 stops at the same cycle as pin 2 (failing cycle 7, the 1st lowest cycle that reaches the maxFailsPerPin limit). | 605 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.13.01.03_SmartRDI_7.10.1.md` | `354688.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| Category \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|⏎\| [DC](<02.07.13.12.02_Setting_up_DC_Scale_pins_with_SmartRDI.md>) \| New static commands for setting DC Scale pins:<br><br>- [forceDropWarn()](<02.07.13.12.02.09_forceDropWarn.md>)<br>- [hiAccuracy()](<02.07.13.12.02.10_hiAccuracy.md>)<br>- [iClampTimeToAlarm()](<02.07.13.12.02.13_iClampTimeToAlarm.md>)<br>- [iClampTimeToDisconnect()](<02.07.13.12.02.14_iClampTimeToDisconnect.md>)<br>- [loadStepMode()](<02.07.13.12.02.27_loadStepMode.md>)<br>- [pulldown()](<02.07.13.12.02.33_pulldown.md>)<br>- [returnDropWarn()](<02.07.13.12.02.37_returnDropWarn.md>)<br><br>In SmartRDI 7.10.1, these member functions only support [DC Scale XPS256 card](<../02.05_Test_Head_Cards/02.05.05.03_DC_Scale_XPS256_card.md>) in the [PMU operating mode](<../02.05_Test_Head_Cards/02.05.05.03.10.15_XPS256_programming_-_PMU_operating_mode.md>).<br><br>[iClamp()](<02.07.13.12.02.11_iClamp.md>) for [DC Scale pin setups](<02.07.13.12.02_Setting_up_DC_Scale_pins_with_SmartRDI.md>) supports the HAC IF mode for XPS256 cards. \|` |
| 606 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.13.01.05_SmartRDI_7.6.0.md` | `351513.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| Category \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|⏎\| [Register Access library](<02.07.13.08.12_Register_Access_library.md>) \| The Register Access library can be used in test methods to perform register operations. \|⏎\| [Alarm handling](<02.07.13.09_SmartRDI__Alarm_handling_for_DCVI.md>) \| Alarm check per result ID. See [rdi.alarm().check().execute()](<02.07.13.09.02.02_rdi.alarm__.check__.execute.md>). \|⏎\| AWG \| addWaveform() adds multiple waveforms to an analog set. \|⏎\| [Cogo](<02.07.13.19.02_Sequencer-controlled_searching_with_cogo.md>) \| Before 7.6.0, each search step in a linear search is a subroutine.<br><br>SmartRDI 7.6.0 supports the entire linear search with only one subroutine in pattern. \|⏎\| [DC](<02.07.13.12.02_Setting_up_DC_Scale_pins_with_SmartRDI.md>) \| [vMeasForceDrop()](<02.07.13.12.02.44_vMeasForceDrop.md>) and [vMeasReturnDrop()](<02.07.13.12.02.46_vMeasReturnDrop.md>) support HC channels of [DC Scale DPS128 cards](<../02.05_Test_Head_Cards/02.05.05.01_DC_Scale_DPS128_DPS64_cards.md>). \|⏎\| [EMAP](<02.07.13.16_SmartRDI__Error_map_results_from_user_patterns.md>) \| [rdi.func().startVec()](<02.07.13.16.10_rdi.func__.startVec.md>) and [rdi.func().stopVec()](<02.07.13.16.11_rdi.func__.stopVec.md>) are enhanced to be used by the emap() class for splitting a pattern to create a new partial pattern and get the EMPA result. \|⏎\| [Relays](<02.07.13.14_SmartRDI__Digital_channel_relay_control.md>) \| The "NO" value can be used for opening a type of relay on one site and closing the same type of relay on another in runtime. See [runTimeVal()](<02.07.13.08.03.01_runTimeVal.md>). \|⏎\| [smartVec](<02.07.13.24_SmartRDI__Vector_and_protocol_programming.md>) \| New flag [SMV_IGNORE_COMMENT](<02.07.13.04_RDI_Configure_file.md#_146692__row_SMV_IGNORE_COMMENT>) in the [RDI_Configure file](<02.07.13.04_RDI_Configure_file.md>) to skip comments generated in pattern. \|⏎\| [TMU](<02.07.13.21_SmartRDI__TMU_test_setup.md>) \| [rdi.id().getTmuErrorState()](<02.07.13.21.21_rdi.id__.getTmuErrorState.md>) for retrieving the error state of specified pins and sites. \|⏎\| [Utility line control](<02.07.13.23_SmartRDI__Utility_lines_control.md>) \| Wait time can be added to pins in Pin Configuration for [sequencer‑controlled utility lines](<02.07.13.23.04_Sequencer_Controlled_Utility_Mask_with_SmartRDI.md>). \|` |
| 607 | `02_System_reference/02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.13.01.12_SmartRDI_2.5.1.md` | `343886.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| Category \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|⏎\| SmartVec \| Support pattern generation with per-site data no matter whether the site is enabled or not. See [writeBit()](<02.07.13.24.35_writeBit.md>), [writeData()](<02.07.13.24.36_writeData.md>), [writeParData()](<02.07.13.24.38_writeParData.md>), and [writeVec()](<02.07.13.24.41_writeVec.md>) \|⏎\| DC \| Support inserting a wait pattern as a sublabel to control the waiting time between actions. See the example in [insertSub()](<02.07.13.12.02.20_insertSub.md>). \|⏎\| UTIL \| The default value for UTIL_DYN_MASK_UNSPECIFIED_PIN is changed to TRUE. Therefore, the default behavior of sequencer‑controlled utility is changed. See [RDI_Configure file](<02.07.13.04_RDI_Configure_file.md>). \|` |

A proportion of devices with mismatching test results (flips) out of all devices tested. \|⏎\| R&R specific result fields \| R&R(P Lower Limit) \| The lower limit of the confidence interval of the fail percent at a given confidence level. \|⏎\| R&R specific result fields \| R&R(P Upper Limit) \| The upper limit of the confidence interval of the fail percent at a given confidence level. \|⏎\| R&R specific result fields \| R&R(Contains 0) \| A flag which indicates whether the value 0 falls in the confidence interval, that is, a value range from the P Lower Limit to P Upper Limit. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/151405.htm`。` |
| 698 | `02_System_reference/02.08_Setup_and_Result_Tools/02.08.04.25.03.04.01_Colors_in_shmoo_plots.md` | `141844.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| ![Color_Bar_Compressed.png](<../../../../raw/150028.png>) \| You can also change the bin color assignments by grasping the arrows on the color bar and dragging them up or down. The system assigns failure values to the bins automatically. By moving the arrows you are effectively changing the acceptance criteria of the tested product.<br><br>By dragging the top arrow down you are declaring more partially failed cells as completely failed, thereby increasing the quality of the tested product (more devices are being declared as failed devices).<br><br>By dragging the bottom arrow up you are declaring more partially failed cells as passed cells, thereby reducing the quality of the tested product (more devices are being declared as passed devices). \|⏎\|---\|---\|` |
| 699 | `02_System_reference/02.08_Setup_and_Result_Tools/02.08.04.28_Site_Select_view.md` | `150977.htm` | #2 | 合并格 | `\|  \| Column Title \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\|  \| Site \| Site number. \|⏎\|  \| State \| The currently selected state of a site, which can be changed either using the shortcut menu or using buttons on the Site Select view toolbar. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from the last testflow execution \| Result \| Test result of the current site in the last testflow execution.<br><br>- N/A: Not test has been executed or test results have been cleared.<br>- PASS: This value indicates a passed site in the last testflow execution.<br>- FAIL: This value indicates a failed site in the last testflow execution. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from the last testflow execution \| Soft \| The number of the software bin. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from the last testflow execution \| Hard \| The number of the hardware bin. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from all executed loops of testflow \| Pass# \| Pass number. Total counts of pass results from all testflow execution loops on the current site. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from all executed loops of testflow \| Fail# \| Fail number. Total counts of fail results from all testflow execution loops on the current site. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from all executed loops of testflow \| Sum# \| Pass# + Fail#. Total counts of all test results (both pass and fail) from all testflow execution loops on the current site. \|⏎\| Site's pass or fail results from all executed loops of testflow \| Yield% \| Pass# / Sum#. Percentage of pass results for the current site. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/150977.htm`。` |

| 701 | `02_System_reference/02.08_Setup_and_Result_Tools/02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md` | `342556.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| Name in GUI<br><br>Keyword \| Area \| Description \| Default \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| #1 …#n<br><br>site_control \| Site control \| These values define the site test sequence if flag [Site Control](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_site_control>) is set to OTHER.<br><br>n = Number of defined sites. \|  \|⏎\| Allow Parallel<br><br>force_serial \| Site control \| Determines whether a test suites is executed in parallel or serially. on Test suite is executed in parallel. off Test suite is executed serially. The flag force_serial is inserted in the list of site control parameters if Allow Parallel has been set to off.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: Allow Parallel (force_serial)](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.05_Site_control_flags.md#_100324__s_force_serial>) in [Site control flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.05_Site_control_flags.md>). \| on \|⏎\| buffer_testflow_log<br><br>buffer_testflow_log \| Testflow \| Controls whether the data log is buffered until the end of the testflow.<br><br>on (1) Enable data log buffering. off (0) Disable data log buffering.<br><br>This is an option for event data logging. The flag allows you to temporarily buffer the data log, then carry all the logged data in the buffer into the event data log stream at the end of the testflow. This way you can minimize the overhead time of the data logging. The details and order of the logged data with this option are the same as of the general event data logging.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| Bypass<br><br>bypass \| Test suite \| The test suite is bypassed. The result is assumed as passed, unless either the [set_fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_fail>) flag is also activated, or the test suite has a test level which is regarded as a fail level.<br><br>on Bypass the test suite. off Execute the test suite.<br><br>See also testflow flag [set_bypass_level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_bypass_level>). \| off \|⏎\| calib_age_monitor<br><br>calib_age_monitor \| Data logging \| Controls whether the monitor process checks the calibration data age when the application model command [MONITOR_CALL](<../02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.02.05.02.40_MONITOR_CALL.md>) is executed. on (1) Enable calibration data age check. off (0) Do not check calibration date age. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| check_testmethod_api<br><br>check_testmethod_api \| Testflow \| Controls whether undefined pin names cause fatal errors.<br><br>on (1) Checks the pin name parameter. off (0) Pin name parameter is not checked.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: check_testmethod_api](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md#_20312__s_check_testmethod_api>) in [Testflow flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md>).<br><br>Check Test Method API flag should be set to "on" if Standard Test Data Format (STDF) will be used for test result data analysis.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| current_monitor<br><br>current_monitor \| Data logging \| Controls whether the monitor process checks other power supply mode when the application model command [MONITOR_CALL](<../02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.02.05.02.40_MONITOR_CALL.md>) is executed. on (1) Enable check of the power supply mode. off (0) Disable check of the power supply mode. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| datalog_formatter<br><br>datalog_formatter \| Data logging \| Enables or disables the Data Bridge calls in the application model that pipe all logged data (in generic data format) to the a data formatter. on (1) Enable the Data Bridge calls. off (0) Disable the Data Bridge calls. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| datalog_sample_size<br><br>datalog_sample_size \| Data logging \| Determines the number of devices to be tested before logging data. n Number of devices<br><br>- Minimum: 1<br>- Maximum: 9999<br><br>For example, a data log sample size of 100 results in only the data for each 100th device being logged. Such a feature is useful in statistical process control. The default sample size is 1 (that is, results for every device are logged). \| 1 \|⏎\| debug_analog<br><br>debug_analog \| Testflow \| Activates or deactivates support for debugging analog tests. on (1) The test method debugger is launched after the execution. off (0) No analog debug information will be generated.<br><br>If you turn this flag on when developing multisite testing by using an analog module that is shared amongst multiple sites, the system will check the routing inconsistency before a test execution. \| off (0) \|⏎\| debug_mode<br><br>debug_mode \| Testflow \| The Debug Mode flag enables debug information collection and provides the controls to select the level of debug information to be displayed in the [Report window](<../../01_Getting_started/01.01_SmarTest_Work_Center/01.01.13_Report_window.md>) during debug execution. For information about debugging, see [Debugging a testflow](<02.08.04.32.02.04_Debugging_a_testflow.md>). 0 (Debug off) (0) No debug information is shown. 1 (Display actions) (1) Display actions: All actions performed are displayed in the report window.If logging to the MCDLog file is enabled, every executed testflow is logged to the file, too (see [Using the Trace Function](<../02.06_Command_Reference/02.06.01.03.05_Using_the_Trace_Function.md>)).<br><br>In online mode, the time required for downloading the patterns is also shown.<br><br>2 (Display results) (2) The results of all the test suites that have been executed are displayed in the report window. 3 (Display execution time) (3) The following information will be displayed in the report window:<br><br>- The total execution time<br>- Detailed information for each test level, including preaction, sublevel, postaction and data log times<br>- The number of devices tested<br>- The execution times for each test suite<br>- The execution time of each test suite to be displayed in the respective test suite node.<br>4 (Display SmarTime) (4) Displays a list of testsuite execution times in the order of execution.<br><br>The following information is displayed in the report window:<br><br>- Execution time of each test suite<br>- Number of devices tested<br>- Average execution time of all executed testflow executions<br>- Total execution time of each test suite<br>- For second execution: Additional columns with second execution times and sum of execution times<br>- For more than two executions: Additional columns with minimum and maximum execution times and the times for the most recent execution. \| 0 (Debug off) (0) \|⏎\| diag_monitor<br><br>diag_monitor \| Data logging \| Controls whether the monitor process checks the diagnostics state when the application model command [MONITOR_CALL](<../02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.02.05.02.40_MONITOR_CALL.md>) is executed. on (1) Enable check of the diagnostics state. off (0) Disable check of the diagnostics state. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| dsp_file_enable<br><br>dsp_file_enable \| n/a \| Legacy flag<br><br>Do not use for new device test programs. \| n.a. \|⏎\| Fail per label<br><br>fail_per_label \| Test suite \| If the executed label is a burst, then setting this flag configures the overview error map to record one result for every label of the burst. This allows you to identify the failing labels of the burst. The error map is configured to use the LMAP mode (see the [mode](<../02.06_Command_Reference/02.06.02.02.46_DCRM__DCRM__-__Digital_Compare_Result_Mode.md#_98524__row_mode>) parameter of [DCRM, DCRM? - (Digital Compare Result Mode)](<../02.06_Command_Reference/02.06.02.02.46_DCRM__DCRM__-__Digital_Compare_Result_Mode.md>)) when this flag is set. This results in 8 to 15 break vectors being added, see illustration in the topic [Pass/Fail per label without break vectors](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.13.08.09.02_Pass_Fail_per_label_without_break_vectors.md>). on Record one result for every label of the burst off Disable \| off \|⏎\| Fail value<br><br>value_on_fail \| Test suite \| Determine whether the test suite returns the actual fail value. This flag needs the [Output on fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_output_on_fail>) test suite flag to be activated. on Enable off Disable \| on \|⏎\| FFC enable<br><br>ffc_enable \| Test suite \| Enables or disable logging of the first failing cycles (the first cycles to give an error) for the test suite. on Enables logging of the first failing cycles. off Disable logging of the first failing cycles. \| off \|⏎\| FFV enable<br><br>ffv_enable \| Test suite \| A subflag of [ffc_enable](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_ffc_enable>): Enables or disables logging of the first failing vectors (the first vectors to give an error) for the test suite. on Enables logging of the first failing vectors. off Disable logging of the first failing vectors. \| off \|⏎\| FRG enable<br><br>frg_enable \| Test suite \| A subflag of [ffc_enable](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_ffc_enable>): Enables or disables the fine result granularity. on Enable the fine result granularity. off Disable the fine result granularity.<br><br>If a cycle contains more than one receive strobe the fine result granularity enables you to get result information per receive strobe. If this flag is disabled you only get result information per cycle. There is no information at which strobes of the cycle the failures occurred.<br><br>Note that this flag must not be set for test suites that execute protocol-aware tests. \| off \|⏎\| global_hold<br><br>global_hold \| Testflow \| Setting this flag allows you to execute the test suites step by step. The test system goes into a hold state whenever a test suite has been executed. on (1) Execute the test suites step by step. off (0) Do not activate the hold state.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: global_hold](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md#_20312__s_global_hold>) in [Testflow flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md>).<br><br>We recommend using the single step procedure for testflow debugging.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| global_overon<br><br>global_overon \| Testflow \| Setting this flag allows the testflow to continue when stop bins are marked as overon bins. on (1) The Bin Overon flag determines the system behavior. When the Bin Overon is on, the testflow execution continues. When the Bin Overon is off, the testflow execution stops at that bin. off (0) The testflow execution stops when a fail bin is reached. For more information, refer to [Overon flag](<../02.10_Data_Logging/02.10.03.03.05_Overon_flag.md>).<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to on. \| off (0) \|⏎\| graphic_result_display<br><br>graphic_result_displa \| Data logging \| Controls whether the binning result is displayed graphically after each testflow execution. Depending on your application model , the Wafermap Screen or the Result Display Screen for a package test will be shown as long as the state of the flag is on (default state). on (1) Enable off (0) Disable For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| H/L/I/U (multiple execution)<br><br>frg_single_shot \| Test suite \| A subflag of [frg_enable](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_frg_enable>): Enables or disables the multishot per-edge detailed result mode (high / low / intermediate / unclear). on Enable (Flag frg_single_shot is deleted) off Disable (Flag frg_single_shot is inserted) \| off \|⏎\| Hardware DSP disable<br><br>hw_dsp_disable \| n/a \| Legacy flag<br><br>Do not use for new device test programs. \| n.a. \|⏎\| hidden_datalog_mode<br><br>hidden_datalog_mode \| Data logging \| Enables compatible or full hidden data logging mode provided by the [SmartCalc framework](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.10_SmartCalc_framework.md>). 0 (off) (0) Turns hidden data logging off. 1 (on - compatibility mode) (1)<br><br>Turns hidden data logging in compatibility mode on.<br><br>In this mode, the limits are used that are defined in the [SMC_SYNCHRONIZE()](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.231.43_SMC_SYNCHRONIZE.md>) test suite.<br><br>2 (on - full hidden mode) (2)<br><br>Turns full hidden data logging on, including [LIMIT](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.126_LIMIT.md>) support.<br><br>In this mode, the limits are used that are defined by the calling test suite.<br><br>This is the recommended mode.<br><br>Note that activating the hidden mode (value 1 or 2) may cause missing or unexpected data log entries when you interactively execute test suites based on the SMC framework. We therefore recommend that you do not activate hidden data logging in this case.<br><br>When you are using the concurrent test framework, the hidden_datalog flag also determines whether the test events are logged separately for each test suite (if the flag is turned on) or all events are logged together in the final CCExecution test suite (if the flag is turned off). \| 0 (off) (0) \|⏎\| hold_on_fail<br><br>hold_on_fail \| Testflow \| If you set this flag, the testflow will be put to a "hold" state every time a test suite fails. A window then lets you select whether to continue or abort the testflow. on (1) Hold if a test suite fails. off (0) Continue if a test suite fails.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: hold_on_fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md#_20312__s_hold_on_fail>) in [Testflow flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md>).<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| Hold<br><br>hold \| Test suite \| The testflow is paused after the particular test suite has been executed. on Pause after the test suite has been executed. off Continue the testflow.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: hold and Hold on fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.04_Test_suite_flags.md#_20262__s_hold_and_hold_on_fail>) in [Test suite flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.04_Test_suite_flags.md>). \| off \|⏎\| Hold on fail<br><br>hold_on_fail \| Test suite \| The testflow is paused if the test suite fails. on Pause if test suite has failed. off Continue the testflow.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: hold and Hold on fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.04_Test_suite_flags.md#_20262__s_hold_and_hold_on_fail>) in [Test suite flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.04_Test_suite_flags.md>). \| off \|⏎\| ink_wafer<br><br>ink_wafer \| Data logging \| Used to communicate to the wafer prober whether it will ink dies according to the test results. SmarTest does not take any actions depending on the flag value: off (0) Do not ink the die. delay (1) Ink the die later, for example, when the wafer is put back into the cassette. on (2) Ink the die immediately. offline (3) Ink after testing. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| limits_enable<br><br>limits_enable \| Testflow \| Control whether the pass/fail limits of each test suite are logged (In addition to the pass/fail results). on (1) Enable logging of pass/fail limits. off (0) Disable logging of pass/fail limits.<br><br>If activated, the actual absolute limits during runtime of a test suite containing variables are recorded in the data log, together with information on the primary sets and labels used. This way you can tell what particular level or timing conditions a test suite passed or failed. This is especially useful when using variables and the Equation System to specify setup and pass/fail conditions.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| log_cycles_after<br><br>log_cycles_after \| Testflow \| This parameter determines how many cycles after a failed cycle are logged. n Number of cycles<br><br>- Minimum: 0<br>- Maximum: 9999<br>For optimized throughput, set the value to 0. \| 0 \|⏎\| log_cycles_before<br><br>log_cycles_before \| Testflow \| This parameter determines how many cycles before a failed cycle are logged. n Number of cycles<br><br>- Minimum: 0<br>- Maximum: 9999<br>For optimized throughput, set the value to 0. \| 0 \|⏎\| log_events_enable<br><br>log_events_enable \| Data logging \| Enables or disables event data logging. on (1) Enable event data logging. off (0) Disable event data logging. For examples, see [Activating event data logging](<../02.10_Data_Logging/02.10.01.01.04_Activating_event_data_logging.md>) and [Setting up the data log information](<../02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.17.08_Setting_up_the_data_log_information.md>).<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to on. \| on (1) \|⏎\| Log first<br><br>ffc_on_fail \| Test suite \| A subflag of [ffv enable](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_ffc_enable>). n The number of first failing cycles to be logged. \| 1 \|⏎\| Log Mixed Signal Waveforms<br><br>mx_waves_enable \| Test suite \| Enables or disables logging of mixed signal waveforms for event data logging. on Enable logging of mixed signal waveforms. off Disable logging of mixed signal waveforms. \| off \|⏎\| max_reprobes<br><br>max_reprobes \| Data logging \| Controls how often the tester attempts to re-probe a die if a bin has its reprobe flag set. n The maximum number of re-probes:<br><br>- Minimum: 0<br>- Maximum: 9999<br>For optimized throughput, set the value to 1. \| 1 \|⏎\| multibin_mode<br><br>multibin_mode \| Testflow \| Controls the handling of multibin calls. default (0) Default mode suppress_on_set_pass (1) Setting this values ignores calls to [SET_MULTIBIN](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.212_SET_MULTIBIN.md>) and the setting of the internal multibin variable in [TESTSET().judgeAndLog](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.244_TESTSET.md>) and [SMC_SYNCHRONIZE()](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.231.43_SMC_SYNCHRONIZE.md>) calls when the [set_pass](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_pass>) test suite flag is set.<br><br>This flag does not affect:<br><br>- The [set_pass_level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_pass_level>) testflow flag<br>- The [RESET_MULTIBIN](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.188_RESET_MULTIBIN.md>) command<br>- The [set_fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_fail>) test suite flag<br>- [SMC_TEST()](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.231.45_SMC_TEST.md>) calls (binning happens in the appended SMC_SYNCHRONIZE call) \| default (0) \|⏎\| Output on fail<br><br>output_on_fail \| Test suite \| Determine whether the test suite returns an overall fail message. The flag must be set if you want the output (for example, from a user procedure) sent to the data log file. If the flag is disabled, no value or per-pin measurements will be made. on Output on fail enabled. off Output on fail disabled. \| on \|⏎\| Output on pass<br><br>output_on_pass \| Test suite \| Determine whether the test suite returns an overall pass message. The flag must be set if you want the output (for example, from a user procedure) sent to the data log file. If the flag is disabled, no value or per-pin measurements will be made. on Output on pass enabled. off Output on pass disabled. \| on \|⏎\| parallel_mode<br><br>parallel_mode \| Testflow \| Controls whether test suites for multiple sites are in parallel or serially. on (1) Execute in parallel. off (0) Execute serially. This flag needs the [Output on pass](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_output_on_pass>) test suite flag to be activated. on Enable off Disable \| on \|⏎\| Per pin on fail<br><br>per_pin_on_fail \| Test suite \| Determine whether the test suite returns a fail message per pin. For this purpose, the $HP83000_ROOT/prod_com/lbin/pr_wafer script is called. on (1) Print the wafer map. off (0) Do not print the wafer map. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| set_bypass_level<br><br>set_bypass_level \| Testflow \| This parameter lets you bypass test suites according to their test level. 0 No test suites are bypassed. 1 …9999 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or greater than the set value will be bypassed. -9999 …-1 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or less than the set value will be bypassed. For optimized throughput, set the flag to 0. \| 0 \|⏎\| set_fail_level<br><br>set_fail_level \| Testflow \| This parameter lets you set test suites to fail according to their test level. 0 No test suites are set to fail. 1 …9999 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or greater than the set value will be set to fail. -9999 …-1 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or less than the set value will be set to fail. For optimized throughput, set the flag to 0. \| 0 \|⏎\| set_pass_level<br><br>set_pass_level \| Testflow \| This parameter lets you set test suites to pass according to their test level. 0 No test suites are set to pass. 1 …9999 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or greater than the set value will be set to pass. -9999 …-1 All test suites for which the [Test Level](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_level>) is equal or less than the set value will be set to pass. \| 0 \|⏎\| Set fail<br><br>set_fail \| Test suite \| Force the test suite to fail. on Force the test suite to fail. off Do not change the state of the test suite. \| off \|⏎\| Set pass<br><br>set_pass \| Test suite \| Force the test suite to pass. on Force the test suite to pass. off Do not change the state of the test suite. \| off \|⏎\| Site match mode<br><br>site_match_mode \| Testflow \| Controls whether site matching is performed. off (0) This state means all sites will be started simultaneously (synchronously). on (1) The on state means all sites will be started successively (with a slight delay). auto(on) (2)<br><br>If the pin configuration does not have the definitions of any shared analog or digital modules, this setting is selected.<br><br>This is same as "on".<br><br>This setting is active when an automatic setting and a [narrow test suite selection](<02.08.04.32.02.01.11.01_Narrow_selection_and_wide_selection.md>) was chosen.<br><br>auto(off)<br><br>If the pin configuration has the definitions of any shared analog or digital modules, this setting is selected.<br><br>This is same as "off".<br><br>This setting is active when an automatic setting and a [wide test suite selection](<02.08.04.32.02.01.11.01_Narrow_selection_and_wide_selection.md>) was chosen.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off.<br><br>For testing more than 255 sites at the same time, set the flag to off; otherwise, an error message will be generated.<br><br>Note If the site match mode of individual test suites within the testflow has been set by the Site Match test suite flag, then these test suites are executed according to the settings of the test suite flag regardless of the setting of this testflow flag.CAUTION You must turn off site matching when sharing BADCs over sites, to ensure proper pattern-controlled DC value measurements with the BADCs.<br><br>For more information, refer to [Controlling the site match function](<../02.06_Command_Reference/02.06.03.01.10.04_Controlling_the_site_match_function.md>), [Controlling Site Synchronization](<../../03_Use_cases_and_application_papers/03.02_How_to_do_Multi-Site_Testing/03.02.02.06.09_Controlling_Site_Synchronization.md>) and [Multi-Site Synchronization in a multiport Setup](<../../03_Use_cases_and_application_papers/03.02_How_to_do_Multi-Site_Testing/03.02.02.06.09.01_Multi-Site_Synchronization_in_a_multiport_Setup.md>). \| off (0) \|⏎\| Site Control<br><br>site_control \| Site control \| Controls which sites will be executed in parallel, semi-parallel or serial mode. PARALLEL The test suite is executed in parallel. SERIAL The test suite is executed serially. SEMIPARALLEL The test suite is executed in a semi-parallel manner.<br><br>Parameters [Size and Cycle](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_size_cycle>) are to be defined if the flag is set to SEMIPARALLEL.<br><br>Note We recommend using the test method APIs [FOR_SEMI_PARALLEL_TEST_BEGIN](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.73_FOR_SEMI_PARALLEL_TEST_BEGIN.md>) and [FOR_SEMI_PARALLEL_TEST_END](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.74_FOR_SEMI_PARALLEL_TEST_END.md>) for semi-parallel or customer-defined sequences. OTHER The test suite is executed serially or semi-parallel in a customer-defined sequence.<br><br>Parameters [#1 ... #n](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_site_sequence>) are to be defined if the flag is set to OTHER.<br><br>Further information is available in [Expanded Site Control window](<02.08.04.27.02_Expanded_Site_Control_window.md>), [Testflow Properties view](<02.08.04.32.03.04_Testflow_Properties_view.md>) and [Controlling Multiple Sites](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.03.13_Controlling_Multiple_Sites.md>). \| PARALLEL \|⏎\| Site Match<br><br>site_match \| Site control \| Is used for multisite testing using the analog modules that are shared with multiple sites.<br><br>AUTO Enable auto-setting depending on the pin configuration definitions. on All sites will be started successively (with a slight delay). off All sites will be started simultaneously (synchronously). CAUTION You must turn off site matching when sharing BADC(s) over sites, to ensure proper pattern-controlled DC value measurements with the BADC.For more information, refer to testflow flag [site_match_mode](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_site_match_mode>) in this table. \| AUTO \|⏎\| Size, Cycle<br><br>site_control \| Site control \| Size and Cycle define the site test sequence if flag [Site Control](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_site_control>) is set to SEMIPARALLEL.<br><br>Refer to [Size & Cycle](<02.08.04.27.02_Expanded_Site_Control_window.md#_100119__row_Size_Cycle>) and the examples in [Expanded Site Control window](<02.08.04.27.02_Expanded_Site_Control_window.md>) for information on how to set these parameters.<br><br>Note: Parameter Cycle may be set to unbounded in the [Expanded Site Control window](<02.08.04.27.02_Expanded_Site_Control_window.md>). The corresponding value in the [Testflow file](<02.08.04.32.03.05_Testflow_file.md>) or in the [Test suite properties view](<02.08.04.32.03.04_Testflow_Properties_view.md#_99279__s_Test_suite_properties_view>) is -1.<br><br>In the [Testflow file](<02.08.04.32.03.05_Testflow_file.md>), these values are appended to flag [Site Control](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_site_control>) as parameters. \| Not defined \|⏎\| sqst_mode<br><br>sqst_mode \| Testflow \| Controls overriding user-defined break waveforms to debug tests that pass standalone but not in a testflow. on (1) The user defined break waveforms are temporarily overridden and the drivers are set to tri-state mode between all tests in the testflow. off (0) The user defined break waveforms are not overridden.<br><br>You may encounter tests that pass when executed as standalone but may fail when executed within a testflow. A glitch on the drivers may be present between pattern bursts when viewed with an oscilloscope. If you experience this situation, check the sqst_mode testflow flag.<br><br>Set to off in typical applications to avoid side effects with keep alive and test time.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| state_display<br><br>state_display \| Data logging \| Controls whether the tester state (idle, loading, testing, etc.) is displayed in a dedicated state display window. on (1) Display the tester state. off (0) Do not display the tester state. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| stdf_generation<br><br>stdf_generation \| n/a \| Legacy flag<br><br>Do not use for new device test programs. \| n.a. \|⏎\| temp_monitor<br><br>temp_monitor \| Data logging \| Controls whether the monitor process checks tester temperature when the application model command [MONITOR_CALL](<../02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.02.05.02.40_MONITOR_CALL.md>) is executed. on (1) Check tester temperature. off (0) Do not check tester temperature. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| test_number_enable<br><br>test_number_enable \| Testflow \| Controls whether test numbers are logged and verified. off (0) No test numbers from the test suite are used for data logging. on (1) Test numbers from the test suite are logged. on + validity check (2) Test numbers from the test suite are logged.<br><br>In addition, SmarTest checks whether a test executed with any of the judgeAndLog... functions (or through the [TEST()](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.241_TEST.md>) or [SMC_TEST()](<../02.07_Test_Method_Reference/02.07.04.01.231.45_SMC_TEST.md>) APIs) has the test number 0, a missing test number, or a test number that has already been assigned to another test.<br><br>If any of these conditions occur, the test suite is stopped with a fail result.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to on. \| on (1) \|⏎\| test_number_inc<br><br>test_number_inc \| Testflow \| The test number increment between loop iterations. n<br><br>Number of increment:<br><br>- Minimum: 0<br>- Maximum: 9999<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to 1.<br><br>For additional information, see section [Additional information: test_number_inc](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md#_20312__s_test_number_inc>) in [Testflow flags](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.02_Testflow_flags.md>). \| 1 \|⏎\| Test Level<br><br>test_level \| Test suite \| An integer (0 …9999) you can assign to a test suite. In combination with the [pass](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_pass_level>), [fail](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_fail_level>), and [bypass](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_set_bypass_level>) levels you set in the testflow flags window, you can control the results from each of your test suites. n Test level \| 0 \|⏎\| Test Number<br><br>override_test_number \| Test suite \| Generated by the software if left empty, or follows the expression set by the user. Is only enabled if the testflow flag [test_number_enable](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_test_number_enable>) is set to on. n Integer test number \| n/a \|⏎\| tm_crash_as_fatal<br><br>tm_crash_as_fatal \| Data logging \| This flag controls crashes and unhandled exceptions. off (0) All sites of the current test suite are sent to the [otherwise bin](<02.08.04.32.01.02.06_Bins.md>) if they have not already been binned. All sites include those to be executed and those already executed by the test suite in which the issue happens. on (1) All sites that have not already been binned are aborted and sent to the [otherwise bin](<02.08.04.32.01.02.06_Bins.md>). Lot testing is aborted and needs user interaction to restart production. For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|⏎\| unburst_mode<br><br>unburst_mode \| Testflow \| Controls how burst labels are treated during testflow execution. off (0) All vector labels in the burst label are executed in one run. on fail (1) If a test suite that runs a burst label fails, it is executed again. If the test suite flag [Fail per label](<02.08.04.32.01.02.07.01_Alphabetical_list_of_flags.md#_342556__row_fail_per_label>) is set for a test suite that runs a burst, then only the failing labels are re-executed in the second run. on (2) Like on fail, but for each test suite that runs a burst label and for the first execution. Note Setting this flag to on fail or on increases the test time. Normally in a production test environment, the unburst mode flag is set to off.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| off (0) \|⏎\| use_hw_dsp<br><br>use_hw_dsp \| n/a \| Legacy flag<br><br>Do not use for new device test programs. \| n.a. \|⏎\| warn_as_fail<br><br>warn_as_fail \| Testflow \| Controls whether firmware warnings automatically cause a test to fail. on (1) Any firmware warning will be treated as test fail. off (0) Any firmware warning will not be treated as test fail and the testflow will continue.<br><br>Using the flag allows you to treat a firmware warning as either test fail or as just a warning.<br><br>For optimized throughput, set the flag to off. \| on (1) \|` |

| 703 | `02_System_reference/02.08_Setup_and_Result_Tools/02.08.04.33.02.12_Softscope_function_of_the_Timing_Diagram.md` | `101618.htm` | #0 | 普通 | `\| ![The control area of the Softscope](<../../../../raw/24146.png>) \| The following control elements are available:<br><br>- Zoom (fill menu) refers to the height of the output channels with respect to an input channel.<br>- Pix lets you reduce the number of tests by the softscope. By default, one test is performed for each pixel to be displayed. A Pix value of n results in a test every nth pixel.<br>- Offset (edit box) the value you assign to the midway point of the particular output channel (in the figure below the small 0 is indicating the midpoint of each channel being actually set at -1.5V).<br>- Swing (edit box) the displayed peek to peek voltage swing you assign to the channel.<br>- Level (edit box) the numerical value of the (y-axis) cursor position on your waveform.<br>- Relat. Pos. (fill menu) indicates the relative positions of the y-axis cursor and marker as percentages of their immediately preceding values.<br>- Level Delta indicates the lo/hi (hi/lo) swing of your waveform. \|⏎\|---\|---\|` |

| 708 | `02_System_reference/02.09_Test_Program_Management/02.09.01.03.06.01.13.01_LAN_communication_flow_for_OSAI_handlers.md` | `345869.htm` | #0 | 合并格 | `\| Message from driver \| Message from handler \| Description \|⏎\|---\|---\|---\|⏎\| Lot start: \| Lot start: \| Lot start: \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|SOL` \| The handler sends the lot start command. \|⏎\| `n\\\|SOL=Lot_ID=1...` \|  \| The driver sends the lot information to the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|ASK_START=LotID:1...` \| The handler asks for the confirmation. \|⏎\| `n\\\|ASK_START=OK` \|  \| The driver replies with `OK`. Otherwise, the driver replies with `ERROR` and aborts the execution. \|⏎\| Wait for device: \| Wait for device: \| Wait for device: \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|TESTSITES=X_001+X_...` \| The handler sends the information of test sites. \|⏎\| `n\\\|TESTSITES=OK\\\|ERROR` \|  \| The driver replies the result after parsing the site information. The driver will reply `ERROR` if the site information is not correct and abort the test execution; or end the lot when it has received the lot end command while waiting for devices. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|START` \| The handler sends the test start signal. \|⏎\| `n\\\|START=OK` \|  \| The driver replies with `OK`. If it has received an unexpected message, it will abort the test. \|⏎\| Test result: \| Test result: \| Test result: \|⏎\| `n\\\|TESTRESULTS=...` \|  \| The driver sends the result to the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|TESTRESULTS=...` \| The handler echos the driver command. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|TESTRESULTS=OK\\\|ERROR` \| The handler sends the final result to the driver. If it is `OK`, the driver will continue the testing. If it is not `OK`, the driver will abort the testing. \|⏎\| Lot end: \| Lot end: \| Lot end: \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|EOL` \| The handler sends the command to end the lot. \|⏎\| `n\\\|EOL=OK` \|  \| The driver replies with `OK` to confirm the end of lot. \|⏎\| Pause and resume: \| Pause and resume: \| Pause and resume: \|⏎\| `n\\\|COMMAND=PAUSE`<br><br>`n\\\|COMMAND=RESUEME` \|  \| The driver sends the pause or resume command to stop or restart the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|COMMAND=PAUSE`<br><br>`n\\\|COMMAND=RESUEME` \| The handler echos the pause or resume command. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|COMMAND=OK\\\|ERROR` \| The handler returns the `OK` or `ERROR` result to the driver. \|⏎\| `n\\\|COMMAND=OK\\\|ERROR` \|  \| The driver echos with `OK` or `ERROR`. The driver sends warning if the command result is not `OK`. \|⏎\| Status query: \| Status query: \| Status query: \|⏎\| `n\\\|STATUSH` \|  \| The driver sends the status query command to the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|STATUSH` \| The handler echos the driver command. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|STATUSH=...` \| The handler returns the query result to the driver. \|⏎\| `n\\\|STATUSH=...` \|  \| The driver echos the result. \|⏎\| Set thermos temperature: \| Set thermos temperature: \| Set thermos temperature: \|⏎\| `n\\\|SET_THERMOSTEP=...` \|  \| The driver sends the temperature-control sequence command to the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|SET_THERMOSTEP=...` \| The handler echos the command. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|SET_THERMOSTEP=OK\\\|KO\\\|ERROR...` \| The handler returns the setting result. \|⏎\| `n\\\|SET_THERMOSTEP=OK\\\|KO\\\|ERROR...` \|  \| The driver echos the result.<br><br>The driver will abort the execution if one other result except for `OK` is returned. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|THERMOSTEP_RES=` \| The handler returns the final result to the driver. \|⏎\| `n\\\|THERMOSTEP_RES=` \|  \| The driver echos the final result. \|⏎\| Query thermos temperature: \| Query thermos temperature: \| Query thermos temperature: \|⏎\| `n\\\|QUERY_THERMOSTEP=...` \|  \| The driver sends the query command to the handler. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|QUERY_THERMOSTEP=...` \| The handler echos the command. \|⏎\|  \| `n\\\|QUERY_THERMOSTEP=...` \| The handler returns the query result to the driver. \|⏎\| `n\\\|QUERY_THERMOSTEP=...` \|  \| The driver echos the result. \|⏎⏎> ⚠️ 转录说明（非原文）：本表在上游 HTML 里含合并格（colspan / rowspan）；⏎> 跨格文字已按覆盖范围重复写出（**不是原表里有重复的格子**）。真结构见 `tdc-html/raw/345869.htm`。` |
